import { BlockInfoCard } from "@/components/ui/block-info-card"

<BlockInfoCard type="rootly" color="#6C72C8" />

使用说明 [#使用说明]

Integrate Rootly incident management into workflows. Create and manage incidents, alerts, services, severities, and retrospectives.

工具 [#工具]

rootly_create_incident [#rootly_create_incident]

Create a new incident in Rootly with optional severity, services, and teams.

输入 [#输入]

| 参数                 | 类型      | 必填 | 描述                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------ | ------- | -- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`           | string  | 是  | Rootly API key                                                                                                                            |
| `title`            | string  | 否  | The title of the incident (auto-generated if not provided)                                                                                |
| `summary`          | string  | 否  | A summary of the incident                                                                                                                 |
| `severityId`       | string  | 否  | Severity ID to attach to the incident                                                                                                     |
| `status`           | string  | 否  | Incident status (in\_triage, started, detected, acknowledged, mitigated, resolved, closed, cancelled, scheduled, in\_progress, completed) |
| `kind`             | string  | 否  | Incident kind (normal, normal\_sub, test, test\_sub, example, example\_sub, backfilled, scheduled, scheduled\_sub)                        |
| `serviceIds`       | string  | 否  | Comma-separated service IDs to attach                                                                                                     |
| `environmentIds`   | string  | 否  | Comma-separated environment IDs to attach                                                                                                 |
| `groupIds`         | string  | 否  | Comma-separated team/group IDs to attach                                                                                                  |
| `incidentTypeIds`  | string  | 否  | Comma-separated incident type IDs to attach                                                                                               |
| `functionalityIds` | string  | 否  | Comma-separated functionality IDs to attach                                                                                               |
| `labels`           | string  | 否  | Labels as JSON object, e.g. \{"platform":"osx","version":"1.29"}                                                                          |
| `private`          | boolean | 否  | Create as a private incident (cannot be undone)                                                                                           |

输出 [#输出]

| 参数               | 类型      | 描述                              |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------- |
| `incident`       | object  | The created incident            |
| ↳ `id`           | string  | Unique incident ID              |
| ↳ `sequentialId` | number  | Sequential incident number      |
| ↳ `title`        | string  | Incident title                  |
| ↳ `slug`         | string  | Incident slug                   |
| ↳ `kind`         | string  | Incident kind                   |
| ↳ `summary`      | string  | Incident summary                |
| ↳ `status`       | string  | Incident status                 |
| ↳ `private`      | boolean | Whether the incident is private |
| ↳ `url`          | string  | URL to the incident             |
| ↳ `shortUrl`     | string  | Short URL to the incident       |
| ↳ `severityName` | string  | Severity name                   |
| ↳ `severityId`   | string  | Severity ID                     |
| ↳ `createdAt`    | string  | Creation date                   |
| ↳ `updatedAt`    | string  | Last update date                |
| ↳ `startedAt`    | string  | Start date                      |
| ↳ `mitigatedAt`  | string  | Mitigation date                 |
| ↳ `resolvedAt`   | string  | Resolution date                 |
| ↳ `closedAt`     | string  | Closed date                     |

rootly_get_incident [#rootly_get_incident]

Retrieve a single incident by ID from Rootly.

输入 [#输入-1]

| 参数           | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                 |
| ------------ | ------ | -- | ---------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`     | string | 是  | Rootly API key                     |
| `incidentId` | string | 是  | The ID of the incident to retrieve |

输出 [#输出-1]

| 参数               | 类型      | 描述                              |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------- |
| `incident`       | object  | The incident details            |
| ↳ `id`           | string  | Unique incident ID              |
| ↳ `sequentialId` | number  | Sequential incident number      |
| ↳ `title`        | string  | Incident title                  |
| ↳ `slug`         | string  | Incident slug                   |
| ↳ `kind`         | string  | Incident kind                   |
| ↳ `summary`      | string  | Incident summary                |
| ↳ `status`       | string  | Incident status                 |
| ↳ `private`      | boolean | Whether the incident is private |
| ↳ `url`          | string  | URL to the incident             |
| ↳ `shortUrl`     | string  | Short URL to the incident       |
| ↳ `severityName` | string  | Severity name                   |
| ↳ `severityId`   | string  | Severity ID                     |
| ↳ `createdAt`    | string  | Creation date                   |
| ↳ `updatedAt`    | string  | Last update date                |
| ↳ `startedAt`    | string  | Start date                      |
| ↳ `mitigatedAt`  | string  | Mitigation date                 |
| ↳ `resolvedAt`   | string  | Resolution date                 |
| ↳ `closedAt`     | string  | Closed date                     |

rootly_update_incident [#rootly_update_incident]

Update an existing incident in Rootly (status, severity, summary, etc.).

输入 [#输入-2]

| 参数                    | 类型      | 必填 | 描述                                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------- | ------- | -- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`              | string  | 是  | Rootly API key                                                                                                                           |
| `incidentId`          | string  | 是  | The ID of the incident to update                                                                                                         |
| `title`               | string  | 否  | Updated incident title                                                                                                                   |
| `summary`             | string  | 否  | Updated incident summary                                                                                                                 |
| `severityId`          | string  | 否  | Updated severity ID                                                                                                                      |
| `status`              | string  | 否  | Updated status (in\_triage, started, detected, acknowledged, mitigated, resolved, closed, cancelled, scheduled, in\_progress, completed) |
| `kind`                | string  | 否  | Incident kind (normal, normal\_sub, test, test\_sub, example, example\_sub, backfilled, scheduled, scheduled\_sub)                       |
| `private`             | boolean | 否  | Set incident as private (cannot be undone)                                                                                               |
| `serviceIds`          | string  | 否  | Comma-separated service IDs                                                                                                              |
| `environmentIds`      | string  | 否  | Comma-separated environment IDs                                                                                                          |
| `groupIds`            | string  | 否  | Comma-separated team/group IDs                                                                                                           |
| `incidentTypeIds`     | string  | 否  | Comma-separated incident type IDs to attach                                                                                              |
| `functionalityIds`    | string  | 否  | Comma-separated functionality IDs to attach                                                                                              |
| `labels`              | string  | 否  | Labels as JSON object, e.g. \{"platform":"osx","version":"1.29"}                                                                         |
| `mitigationMessage`   | string  | 否  | How was the incident mitigated?                                                                                                          |
| `resolutionMessage`   | string  | 否  | How was the incident resolved?                                                                                                           |
| `cancellationMessage` | string  | 否  | Why was the incident cancelled?                                                                                                          |

输出 [#输出-2]

| 参数               | 类型      | 描述                              |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------- |
| `incident`       | object  | The updated incident            |
| ↳ `id`           | string  | Unique incident ID              |
| ↳ `sequentialId` | number  | Sequential incident number      |
| ↳ `title`        | string  | Incident title                  |
| ↳ `slug`         | string  | Incident slug                   |
| ↳ `kind`         | string  | Incident kind                   |
| ↳ `summary`      | string  | Incident summary                |
| ↳ `status`       | string  | Incident status                 |
| ↳ `private`      | boolean | Whether the incident is private |
| ↳ `url`          | string  | URL to the incident             |
| ↳ `shortUrl`     | string  | Short URL to the incident       |
| ↳ `severityName` | string  | Severity name                   |
| ↳ `severityId`   | string  | Severity ID                     |
| ↳ `createdAt`    | string  | Creation date                   |
| ↳ `updatedAt`    | string  | Last update date                |
| ↳ `startedAt`    | string  | Start date                      |
| ↳ `mitigatedAt`  | string  | Mitigation date                 |
| ↳ `resolvedAt`   | string  | Resolution date                 |
| ↳ `closedAt`     | string  | Closed date                     |

rootly_list_incidents [#rootly_list_incidents]

List incidents from Rootly with optional filtering by status, severity, and more.

输入 [#输入-3]

| 参数             | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                                                                                                                         |
| -------------- | ------ | -- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `apiKey`       | string | 是  | Rootly API key                                                                                                                             |
| `status`       | string | 否  | Filter by status (in\_triage, started, detected, acknowledged, mitigated, resolved, closed, cancelled, scheduled, in\_progress, completed) |
| `severity`     | string | 否  | Filter by severity slug                                                                                                                    |
| `search`       | string | 否  | Search term to filter incidents                                                                                                            |
| `services`     | string | 否  | Filter by service slugs (comma-separated)                                                                                                  |
| `teams`        | string | 否  | Filter by team slugs (comma-separated)                                                                                                     |
| `environments` | string | 否  | Filter by environment slugs (comma-separated)                                                                                              |
| `sort`         | string | 否  | Sort order (e.g., -created\_at, created\_at, -started\_at)                                                                                 |
| `pageSize`     | number | 否  | Number of items per page (default: 20)                                                                                                     |
| `pageNumber`   | number | 否  | Page number for pagination                                                                                                                 |

输出 [#输出-3]

| 参数               | 类型      | 描述                                 |
| ---------------- | ------- | ---------------------------------- |
| `incidents`      | array   | List of incidents                  |
| ↳ `id`           | string  | Unique incident ID                 |
| ↳ `sequentialId` | number  | Sequential incident number         |
| ↳ `title`        | string  | Incident title                     |
| ↳ `slug`         | string  | Incident slug                      |
| ↳ `kind`         | string  | Incident kind                      |
| ↳ `summary`      | string  | Incident summary                   |
| ↳ `status`       | string  | Incident status                    |
| ↳ `private`      | boolean | Whether the incident is private    |
| ↳ `url`          | string  | URL to the incident                |
| ↳ `shortUrl`     | string  | Short URL to the incident          |
| ↳ `severityName` | string  | Severity name                      |
| ↳ `severityId`   | string  | Severity ID                        |
| ↳ `createdAt`    | string  | Creation date                      |
| ↳ `updatedAt`    | string  | Last update date                   |
| ↳ `startedAt`    | string  | Start date                         |
| ↳ `mitigatedAt`  | string  | Mitigation date                    |
| ↳ `resolvedAt`   | string  | Resolution date                    |
| ↳ `closedAt`     | string  | Closed date                        |
| `totalCount`     | number  | Total number of incidents returned |

Create a new alert in Rootly for on-call notification and routing.

输入 [#输入-4]

| 参数                 | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                                                      |
| ------------------ | ------ | -- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`           | string | 是  | Rootly API key                                                          |
| `summary`          | string | 是  | The summary of the alert                                                |
| `description`      | string | 否  | A detailed description of the alert                                     |
| `source`           | string | 是  | The source of the alert (e.g., api, manual, datadog, pagerduty)         |
| `status`           | string | 否  | Alert status on creation (open, triggered)                              |
| `serviceIds`       | string | 否  | Comma-separated service IDs to attach                                   |
| `groupIds`         | string | 否  | Comma-separated team/group IDs to attach                                |
| `environmentIds`   | string | 否  | Comma-separated environment IDs to attach                               |
| `externalId`       | string | 否  | External ID for the alert                                               |
| `externalUrl`      | string | 否  | External URL for the alert                                              |
| `deduplicationKey` | string | 否  | Alerts sharing the same deduplication key are treated as a single alert |

输出 [#输出-4]

| ↳ `source`           | string | Alert source      |
| ↳ `status`           | string | Alert status      |
| ↳ `externalId`       | string | External ID       |
| ↳ `externalUrl`      | string | External URL      |
| ↳ `deduplicationKey` | string | Deduplication key |
| ↳ `createdAt`        | string | Creation date     |
| ↳ `updatedAt`        | string | Last update date  |
| ↳ `startedAt`        | string | Start date        |
| ↳ `endedAt`          | string | End date          |

| `actionItemId` | string | 是  | The ID of the action item to delete |

输出 [#输出-35]

| 参数        | 类型      | 描述                                  |
| --------- | ------- | ----------------------------------- |
| `success` | boolean | Whether the action item was deleted |
| `message` | string  | Result message                      |

rootly_snooze_alert [#rootly_snooze_alert]

Snooze a Rootly alert for a set number of minutes.

输入 [#输入-36]

| 参数             | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                    |
| -------------- | ------ | -- | ------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`       | string | 是  | Rootly API key                        |
| `alertId`      | string | 是  | The ID of the alert to snooze         |
| `delayMinutes` | number | 是  | Number of minutes to snooze the alert |

输出 [#输出-36]

| 参数                   | 类型     | 描述                |
| -------------------- | ------ | ----------------- |
| `alert`              | object | The snoozed alert |
| ↳ `id`               | string | Unique alert ID   |
| ↳ `shortId`          | string | Short alert ID    |
| ↳ `summary`          | string | Alert summary     |
| ↳ `description`      | string | Alert description |
| ↳ `source`           | string | Alert source      |
| ↳ `status`           | string | Alert status      |
| ↳ `externalId`       | string | External ID       |
| ↳ `externalUrl`      | string | External URL      |
| ↳ `deduplicationKey` | string | Deduplication key |
| ↳ `createdAt`        | string | Creation date     |
| ↳ `updatedAt`        | string | Last update date  |
| ↳ `startedAt`        | string | Start date        |
| ↳ `endedAt`          | string | End date          |

rootly_escalate_alert [#rootly_escalate_alert]

Escalate a Rootly alert, optionally to a specific escalation policy or level.

输入 [#输入-37]

| 参数                      | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                                                             |
| ----------------------- | ------ | -- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `apiKey`                | string | 是  | Rootly API key                                                                 |
| `alertId`               | string | 是  | The ID of the alert to escalate                                                |
| `escalationPolicyId`    | string | 否  | Escalation policy ID to escalate to (use List Escalation Policies to find IDs) |
| `escalationPolicyLevel` | number | 否  | Escalation policy level to escalate to                                         |

输出 [#输出-37]

| 参数                   | 类型     | 描述                  |
| -------------------- | ------ | ------------------- |
| `alert`              | object | The escalated alert |
| ↳ `id`               | string | Unique alert ID     |
| ↳ `shortId`          | string | Short alert ID      |
| ↳ `summary`          | string | Alert summary       |
| ↳ `description`      | string | Alert description   |
| ↳ `source`           | string | Alert source        |
| ↳ `status`           | string | Alert status        |
| ↳ `externalId`       | string | External ID         |
| ↳ `externalUrl`      | string | External URL        |
| ↳ `deduplicationKey` | string | Deduplication key   |
| ↳ `createdAt`        | string | Creation date       |
| ↳ `updatedAt`        | string | Last update date    |
| ↳ `startedAt`        | string | Start date          |
| ↳ `endedAt`          | string | End date            |

rootly_list_incident_events [#rootly_list_incident_events]

List the timeline events for a Rootly incident.

输入 [#输入-38]

| 参数           | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                     |
| ------------ | ------ | -- | -------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`     | string | 是  | Rootly API key                         |
| `incidentId` | string | 是  | The ID of the incident                 |
| `pageSize`   | number | 否  | Number of items per page (default: 20) |
| `pageNumber` | number | 否  | Page number for pagination             |

输出 [#输出-38]

| 参数             | 类型     | 描述                                      |
| -------------- | ------ | --------------------------------------- |
| `events`       | array  | List of incident timeline events        |
| ↳ `id`         | string | Unique event ID                         |
| ↳ `event`      | string | The event description                   |
| ↳ `visibility` | string | Event visibility (internal or external) |
| ↳ `occurredAt` | string | When the event occurred                 |
| ↳ `createdAt`  | string | Creation date                           |
| ↳ `updatedAt`  | string | Last update date                        |
| `totalCount`   | number | Total number of events returned         |

rootly_run_workflow [#rootly_run_workflow]

Trigger a Rootly automation workflow, optionally scoped to an incident or alert.

输入 [#输入-39]

| 参数                | 类型      | 必填 | 描述                                                                             |
| ----------------- | ------- | -- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `apiKey`          | string  | 是  | Rootly API key                                                                 |
| `workflowId`      | string  | 是  | The ID of the workflow to run                                                  |
| `incidentId`      | string  | 否  | Incident ID to run the workflow against                                        |
| `alertId`         | string  | 否  | Alert ID to run the workflow against                                           |
| `immediate`       | boolean | 否  | Run immediately (true) or respect the workflow wait time (false). Default true |
| `checkConditions` | boolean | 否  | Whether to evaluate the workflow conditions before running. Default false      |

输出 [#输出-39]

| 参数                | 类型     | 描述                                                                                 |
| ----------------- | ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `workflowRun`     | object | The triggered workflow run                                                         |
| ↳ `id`            | string | Unique workflow run ID                                                             |
| ↳ `workflowId`    | string | ID of the workflow that ran                                                        |
| ↳ `status`        | string | Run status (queued, started, completed, completed\_with\_errors, failed, canceled) |
| ↳ `statusMessage` | string | Status detail message                                                              |
| ↳ `triggeredBy`   | string | What triggered the run (system, user, workflow)                                    |
| ↳ `incidentId`    | string | Associated incident ID                                                             |
| ↳ `alertId`       | string | Associated alert ID                                                                |
| ↳ `startedAt`     | string | When the run started                                                               |
| ↳ `completedAt`   | string | When the run completed                                                             |
| ↳ `failedAt`      | string | When the run failed                                                                |
| ↳ `canceledAt`    | string | When the run was canceled                                                          |
